    WHEN [u].[Id] IS NOT NULL THEN COALESCE([u].[FirstName], N'') + N' ' + COALESCE([u].[LastName], N'')
    ELSE N'Unknown'
END AS [LeadName], [e0].[TemplateName], [u0].[FirstName] + N' ' + [u0].[LastName] AS [SentByName], [e].[SentDate], [e].[Status]
FROM [EmailLogs] AS [e]
INNER JOIN [Campaigns] AS [c] ON [e].[CampaignID] = [c].[CampaignID]
INNER JOIN [Leads] AS [l] ON [e].[LeadID] = [l].[LeadID]
LEFT JOIN [Users] AS [u] ON [l].[UserID] = [u].[Id]
INNER JOIN [EmailTemplates] AS [e0] ON [e].[TemplateID] = [e0].[TemplateID]
INNER JOIN [Users] AS [u0] ON [e].[SentBy] = [u0].[Id]
ORDER BY [e].[SentDate] DESC
